117	
118	def _extract(dataset, regime, metric):
119	    """Return (sizes, values) for non-None entries."""
120	    sizes, vals = [], []
121	    for sz in ALL_SIZES:
122	        key = (sz, regime, dataset)
123	        if key in DATA and DATA[key].get(metric) is not None:
124	            sizes.append(sz)
125	            vals.append(DATA[key][metric])
126	    return np.array(sizes), np.array(vals)
127	
128	
129	def _fmt_xticks(ax):
130	    ax.set_xscale("log")
131	    ax.set_xticks(ALL_SIZES)
132	    ax.get_xaxis().set_major_formatter(mticker.FuncFormatter(
133	        lambda x, _: f"{int(x)}B" if x == int(x) else f"{x}B"))
134	    ax.get_xaxis().set_minor_formatter(mticker.NullFormatter())
135	    ax.set_xlim(ALL_SIZES[0] * 0.65, ALL_SIZES[-1] * 1.5)
136	    ax.tick_params(axis="x", which="minor", bottom=False)
137	    for label in ax.get_xticklabels():
138	        label.set_rotation(45)
139	        label.set_ha("right")
140	
141	
142	# ─── FIGURE A: Signal ρ vs Model Size ────────────────────────────────────────
143	
144	def plot_signal_rho(outdir: Path):
145	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(6.75, 2.2), sharey=True)
146	
147	    for ax, ds in zip(axes, DATASETS):
148	        for signal in ["lp_rho", "sj_rho", "vc_rho"]:
149	            for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():
150	                sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, signal)
151	                if len(sx) == 0:
152	                    continue
153	                label = f"{SIGNAL_LABELS[signal]} ({rlabel})"
154	                ax.plot(sx, sy, color=SIGNAL_COLORS[signal],
155	                        linestyle=lstyle, marker=SIGNAL_MARKERS[signal],
156	                        label=label, zorder=3)
157	
158	        ax.set_title(ds)
159	        _fmt_xticks(ax)
160	        ax.set_xlabel("Model Size")
161	        ax.axhline(0, color="grey", lw=0.5, ls=":")
162	        ax.grid(True, axis="y")
163	
164	    axes[0].set_ylabel("Spearman ρ")
165	
166	    handles, labels = [], []
167	    for ax in axes:
168	        h, l = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()
169	        for hi, li in zip(h, l):
170	            if li not in labels:
171	                handles.append(hi)
172	                labels.append(li)
173	    fig.legend(handles, labels, loc="upper center", ncol=3,
174	               bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.13), frameon=False)
175	
176	    fig.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 0.92])
177	    for ext in ("pdf", "png"):
178	        fig.savefig(outdir / f"scaling_signal_rho.{ext}")
179	    plt.close(fig)
180	    print(f"  → scaling_signal_rho.pdf/png")
181	
182	
183	# ─── FIGURE B: Degeneracy vs Model Size ──────────────────────────────────────
184	
185	def plot_degeneracy(outdir: Path):

191	            if len(sx) == 0:
192	                continue
193	            label = f"{ds} ({rlabel})"
194	            ax.plot(sx, sy, color=DATASET_COLORS[ds],
195	                    linestyle=lstyle, marker=DATASET_MARKERS[ds],
196	                    label=label, zorder=3)
197	
198	    _fmt_xticks(ax)
199	    ax.set_xlabel("Model Size")
200	    ax.set_ylabel("Degeneracy Rate (%)")

257	                    continue

259	                        linestyle=lstyle, marker=SIGNAL_MARKERS[signal],

261	        ax.set_title(ds)

279	                h_sig.append(line)

292	                       linestyle=lstyle, marker=DATASET_MARKERS[ds],

299

321	    for ds in DATASETS:

323	            sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, "construction_lp_delta")

[\"lp_rho\", \"sj_rho\", \"vc_rho\"]:\n            for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():\n                sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, signal)\n                if len(sx) == 0:\n                    continue\n                ax.plot(sx, sy, color=SIGNAL_COLORS[signal],\n                        linestyle=lstyle, marker=SIGNAL_MARKERS[signal],\n                        label=f\"{SIGNAL_LABELS[signal]} ({rlabel})\", zorder=3)\n        ax.set_title(ds)\n        _fmt_xticks(ax)\n        ax.axhline(0, color=\"grey\", lw=0.5, ls=\":\")\n        ax.grid(True, axis=\"y\")\n        if col == 0:\n            ax.set_ylabel(\"Spearman ρ\")\n        if col == 1:\n            ax.set_xlabel(\"Model Size\")\n\n    # Row 1 legend\n    h_sig, l_sig = [], []\n    for signal in [\"lp_rho\", \"sj_rho\", \"vc_rho\"]:\n        for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():\n            lab = f\"{SIGNAL_LABELS[signal]} ({rlabel})\"\n            if lab not in l_sig:\n                line = plt.Line2D([], [], color=SIGNAL_COLORS[signal],\n                                  linestyle=lstyle, marker=SIGNAL_MARKERS[signal],\n                                  markersize=4, lw=1.2)\n                h_sig.append(line)\n                l_sig.append(lab)\n    fig.legend(h_sig, l_sig, loc=\"upper center\", ncol=3,\n               bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.99), frameon=False, fontsize=6.5)\n\n    # Row 2: Degeneracy\n    ax_dg = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0:2])\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():\n            sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, \"degen\")\n            if len(sx) == 0:\n                continue\n            ax_dg.plot(sx, sy, color=DATASET_COLORS[ds],\n                       linestyle=lstyle, marker=DATASET_MARKERS[ds],\n                       label=f\"{ds} ({rlabel})\", zorder=3)\n    _fmt_xticks(ax_dg)\n    ax_dg.set_xlabel(\"Model Size\")\n    ax_dg.set_ylabel(\"Degeneracy Rate (%)\")\n    ax_dg.grid(True, axis=\"y\")\n    ax_dg.legend(loc=\"best\", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=6.5)\n\n    # Row 2 right: empty or greedy F1 bonus\n    ax_f1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 2])\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():\n            sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, \"greedy_f1\")\n            if len(sx) == 0:\n                continue\n            ax_f1.plot(sx, sy * 100, color=DATASET_COLORS[ds],\n                       linestyle=lstyle, marker=DATASET_MARKERS[ds],\n                       label=f\"{ds} ({rlabel})\", zorder=3)\n    _fmt_xticks(ax_f1)\n    ax_f1.set_xlabel(\"Model Size\")\n    ax_f1.set_ylabel(\"Greedy F1 (%)\")\n    ax_f1.grid(True, axis=\"y\")\n    ax_f1.legend(loc=\"best\", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=6.5)\n\n    # Row 3: Construction ΔF1\n    ax_cd = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 0:2])\n    ax_cd.axhline(0, color=\"grey\", lw=0.8, ls=\"-\", zorder=1)\n    ax_cd.axhspan(0, 10, color=\"#c8e6c9\", alpha=0.2, zorder=0)\n    ax_cd.axhspan(-5, 0, color=\"#ffcdd2\", alpha=0.2, zorder=0)\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for regime, (rlabel, lstyle, _) in REGIMES.items():\n            sx, sy = _extract(ds, regime, \"construction_lp_delta\")\n            if len(sx) == 0:\n                continue\n            ax_cd.plot(sx, sy, color=DATASET_COLORS[ds],\n                       linestyle=lstyle, marker=DATASET_MARKERS[ds],\n                       label=f\"{ds} ({rlabel})\", zorder=3)\n    _fmt_xticks(ax_cd)\n    ax_cd.set_xlabel(\"Model Size\")\n    ax_cd.set_ylabel(\"Construction ΔF1 (pp)\")\n    ax_cd.grid(True, axis=\"y\")\n    ax_cd.legend(loc=\"best\", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=6.5)\n\n    for ext in (\"pdf\", \"png\"):\n        fig.savefig(outdir / f\"scaling_curve_draft.{ext}\")\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  → scaling_curve_draft.pdf/png\")\n\n\n# ─── MAIN ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nif __name__ ```text
1	\section{Threshold Regime Analysis}\label{sec:theory_scaling}
2	
3	\subsection{Entity Construction as MBR Decoding}\label{sec:mbr_theory}
4	
5	Entity-level construction selects $\hat{y}_{\textup{MBR}} = \{e \in E_\cup : \hat{p}(e) > \alpha/(\alpha{+}1)\}$ under balanced set-overlap utility; $\alpha{=}1$ recovers majority vote (Proposition~\ref{prop:construction_mbr}; Table~\ref{tab:entity_construction}; Appendix~\ref{app:mbr_proof}).
6	Signal-derived weights $w_i$ yield quality-weighted MBR: $\hat{p}_w(e) = \sum_{i} w_i \, \mathbf{1}[e {\in} y_i] / \sum_{i} w_i$; weighting improves when $w_i$ correlates with instance quality but degrades otherwise (LP on SciERC: $\rho{\approx}0.2$; \S\ref{sec:prescriptive}), consistent with utility degradation on structured outputs \cite{eikema2025structured}.
7	We select $\varepsilon{=}0.05$ at the elbow of the compression curve, where LP-best selection advantage transitions from $+1.5$\,pp (compressed) to $+4.6$\,pp (non-compressed; Appendix~\ref{app:fewnerd_epsilon}).
8	
9	\subsection{Scaling and Inverse Scaling}\label{sec:scaling}
10	
11	Oracle F1 follows $F_1(N) = a - b \cdot N^{-c}$ ($R^2 > 0.999$; Figure~\ref{fig:oracle_scaling}): Few-NERD ($c{\approx}0.61$) scales comparably to reasoning tasks \cite{brown2024monkeys}, SciERC converges more slowly ($c{\approx}0.35$), yet confidence-based aggregation recovers ${<}1$\,pp---a persistent gap.
12	The gap persists across scales and families: scaling from 8B to 14B FT increases oracle headroom ($+1.2$\,pp) with minimal greedy gain ($+0.9$\,pp), and GPT-5.5 (200 Few-NERD instances, zero-shot) exhibits the same pattern---50\% degeneracy, SJ $\rho{=}0.346$, MV $+1.4$\,pp yet 6.8\,pp oracle headroom unrealized.\footnote{Of the 10.1\% of GPT-5.5 samples producing empty entity sets, 88\% correspond to gold-empty instances; true parse failures affect only 1.2\% of samples (19/1{,}600) and shift all reported metrics by ${<}0.5$\,pp. See Appendix~\ref{app:frontier_sc}.}
13	Degeneracy is model-family-dependent rather than scale-dependent (Qwen2.5: $31.7$--$46.2$\% vs.\ Qwen3: $13.0$\% vs.\ GPT-5.5: $50$\% on Few-NERD; Appendix~\ref{app:cross_scale}).
14	
15	\begin{figure}[t]
16	\centering
17	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_oracle_scaling.pdf}
18	\caption{Oracle $F_1$ as a function of sample count $N$, fitted with $F_1(N) = a - b \cdot N^{-c}$. All fits $R^2 > 0.999$. Horizontal dashed lines: greedy ($T{=}0$) F1. The ceiling grows monotonically with $N$, but the correlation-selection gap (\S\ref{sec:selection_gap}) limits how much is actually recoverable.}
19	\label{fig:oracle_scaling}
20	\end{figure}
21	
22	\paragraph{Inverse scaling under adaptive thresholds.}
23	Increasing $N$ \textit{decreases} construction F1 in 40 of 58 transitions ($p{<}0.05$; Appendix~\ref{app:n_scaling}).
24	Methods using $\theta{=}2/N$ are most affected ($-3.09$\,pp at $N{=}8$, $-6.90$\,pp at $N{=}16$ vs.\ greedy on Few-NERD); majority vote ($\theta{=}\tfrac{1}{2}$) reduces the delta to $-0.87$/$-0.60$\,pp---severity reduction, not immunity (Table~\ref{tab:theta_sweep}).
25	Inverse scaling concentrates in non-degenerate instances ($11.1$\% vs.\ $1.3$\% for degenerate; OR${=}0.109$; Appendix~\ref{app:degen_inverse}).
26	Few-shot demonstrations improve QE (CoNLL SJ $\rho$ $.30{\to}.44$) but not construction (all n.s.; Appendix~\ref{app:fs_error}).
27	
28	\subsection{Degeneracy Decomposition}\label{sec:theory}
29	
30	\noindent\textbf{Degeneracy Decomposition.}\label{thm:degeneracy_bound}
31	For any aggregation method $A$: $\mathbb{E}[\Delta F_1(A)] \leq (1 - D) \cdot G_{\textup{nd}}$,
32	where $D$ is the degeneracy rate and $G_{\textup{nd}}$ the oracle gap on non-degenerate instances.
33	For signal-based methods with alignment $\alpha \in [0,1]$, this tightens to $(1{-}D) \cdot G_{\textup{nd}} \cdot \alpha$ (proof in Appendix~\ref{app:bound_proof}).
34	
35	\noindent The bound is an intentionally loose \textit{necessary condition test}: when $D{\to}1$ or $\alpha{\to}0$, no aggregation method can improve over greedy, regardless of implementation.
36	It identifies \textit{where} aggregation fails---screening for favorable conditions---rather than quantitatively predicting gains.
37	Across 16 configurations, all gains respect the bound; the low recovery ratio (3--12\%; Figure~\ref{fig:bound}) reflects its role as a screening test, not a tight estimate.
38	$\alpha$---not $D$---is the binding constraint ($\alpha{=}0.000$--$0.121$; $8$--$33{\times}$ tighter), and this conclusion is robust to signal combination (\S\ref{sec:robustness_main}), verifier-based construction (\S\ref{sec:verifier}), and $\theta_{\text{crit}} \in [0.60, 0.80]$.
39	
40	\begin{figure}[t]
41	\centering
42	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig_bound}
43	\caption{SC gain vs.\ degeneracy across 16 configurations (SciERC-specific $\alpha$). Red: signal-adjusted bound ($\alpha{=}0.068$); vertical dashed: $\theta_{\text{crit}}{=}0.70$; horizontal dotted: $\varepsilon{=}0.3$\,pp. All positive gains fall within the bound (right panel: ratio to weak bound, all ${<}0.13$).}
44	\label{fig:bound}
45	\end{figure}
46	
47	\paragraph{Bound looseness.}
48	The decomposition is a necessary condition screen showing how degeneracy and alignment jointly suppress gains, not a quantitative prediction tool. Two sources of looseness dominate: (1)~the entity independence assumption---split-half correlation $r{=}0.395$--$0.578$ (Table~\ref{tab:fk_independence}) reveals substantial inter-entity correlation structure within instances, which the bound ignores; (2)~$\alpha$ is estimated conservatively as the minimum across instances rather than an instance-adaptive value, further widening the gap. Together these explain the 3--12\% recovery ratio (bound ratio $0.13$--$0.63$). The decomposition nonetheless identifies $\alpha{\to}0$, not degeneracy, as the binding constraint across all tested configurations.
49	
50	MV improves F1 only if marginal-entity precision exceeds $F_1^{\textup{gr}}/2$ (Proposition~\ref{prop:entity_selection_bound}; Appendix~\ref{app:alpha_crit}).
51	Table~\ref{tab:alpha_crit} validates: at $N{=}8$, no dataset meets this condition ($\Delta$ from $-2.74$ to ${\approx}0$\,pp); CoNLL ($\alpha_{\textup{crit,eff}}{=}1.148 {>} 1$) makes MV gain structurally impossible.
52	
```

1490	\bottomrule
1491	\end{tabular}
1492	\end{table}
1493	
1494	Signal rankings are consistent across our 3 seeds (Kendall's $W{=}1.000$ for NER at $N{=}8$, both models), dropping to $W{=}0.956$ at $N{=}16$ where SJ and VC converge (Table~\ref{tab:ranking-stability}); however, with only $n{=}3$ seeds statistical power is insufficient to confirm stability, and additional seeds may reveal ranking changes.
1495	Doubling $N$ reduces cross-seed $\sigma$ by 32\% on NER and 24\% on RE (Table~\ref{tab:sigma-comparison}), with the largest reductions for SJ ($-43\%$ NER) and EM ($-59\%$ NER); additional samples stabilize structural signals more than LP.
1496	
1497	\paragraph{$N$-scaling: selection headroom vs.\ selection gain.}
1498	Table~\ref{tab:n_scaling_selection} traces the effect of sample count on degeneracy, oracle headroom, and per-signal selection gain.
1499	Oracle headroom grows roughly linearly with $N$ ($+4.4$~pp at $N{=}2$ to $+20.0$~pp at $N{=}32$).
1500	LP selection improves monotonically with $N$ ($+0.39$~pp at $N{=}8$ to $+0.95$~pp at $N{=}32$), but even at $N{=}32$, where degeneracy falls to 4\% and oracle headroom reaches ${\sim}20$~pp, the best signal captures less than 5\% of the available improvement.
1501	Degeneracy drops monotonically (43\% at $N{=}2$ to 4\% at $N{=}32$), yet reduced degeneracy does not unlock selection, consistent with the LoRA-rank ablation finding (\S\ref{sec:robustness_main}).
1502	VC selection degrades with increasing $N$ ($-2.66$~pp at $N{=}2$ to $-4.35$~pp at $N{=}32$), consistent with mode-seeking bias in voting-based selection as the candidate pool grows.
1503	
1504	\paragraph{Few-NERD $N$-scaling: MV at $N{=}16$.}
1505	On Few-NERD (Qwen3-8B, 3-seed), increasing from $N{=}8$ to $N{=}16$ reduces degeneracy ($8.8$\%$\to$$4.0{\pm}1.4$\%) and attenuates MV degradation ($-2.74$\,pp$\to$$-1.65{\pm}0.61$\,pp), but MV remains negative.
1506	Additional samples reduce the MV penalty but do not reverse it, consistent with the correlated-error mechanism: more samples provide better frequency estimates but cannot overcome systematic shared mistakes.
1507	SJ $\rho$ is used for quality estimation at $N{=}16$ (FK conditional $\rho$ shows sign instability across seeds: mean${=}0.051{\pm}0.062$).
1508	
1509	\begin{table}[t]
1510	\centering
1511	\small
1512	\caption{Effect of sample count on selection (SciERC NER, Qwen3-8B, $T{=}1.0$, gold-filtered, seed${=}42$; exp\_025).\protect\footnotemark\ ``Best $\Delta$'': highest selection gain across all 5 signals. Per-signal $\Delta$ in pp vs.~greedy.}
1513	\label{tab:n_scaling_selection}
1514	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{
1515	\begin{tabular}{ccccccccc}
1516	\toprule
1517	$N$ & Degen.\% & Oracle (pp) & LP & SJ & FK & EM & VC & Best $\Delta$ \\
1518	\midrule
1519	2 & 42.7\% & $+$4.4 & $-$0.89 & $-$2.72 & $-$2.72 & $-$2.72 & $-$2.66 & $-$0.89 \\
1520	4 & 21.0\% & $+$10.2 & $+$0.03 & $-$0.31 & $-$0.40 & $-$0.74 & $-$2.16 & $+$0.03 \\
1521	8 & 11.0\% & $+$14.5 & $+$0.39 & $+$0.20 & $+$0.15 & $-$0.77 & $-$2.87 & $+$0.39 \\
1522	16 & 7.4\% & $+$17.7 & $+$0.78 & $+$0.24 & $-$0.02 & $-$0.06 & $-$3.95 & $+$0.78 \\
1523	32 & 4.0\% & $+$20.0 & $+$0.95 & $+$0.35 & $+$0.65 & $+$0.22 & $-$4.35 & $+$0.95 \\
1524	\bottomrule
1525	\end{tabular}
1526	}
1527	\end{table}
1528	\footnotetext{$N$-scaling analysis uses gold-filtered evaluation ($n{=}529$, greedy F1${=}0.640$, seed 42). Values differ from Table~\ref{tab:selection} (0.645, 2-seed) and Table~\ref{tab:fewnerd} (0.663, 3-seed) due to seed and aggregation differences; relative trends are consistent.}
1529	
1530	\paragraph{Bootstrap significance at $N{=}32$.}
1531	Table~\ref{tab:bootstrap_n32} reports bootstrap confidence intervals and $p$-values for selection at $N{=}32$ ($B{=}10{,}000$, $n{=}529$).
1532	No signal achieves significant improvement over greedy at $\alpha{=}0.05$ (LP: $p{=}0.091$; FK: $p{=}0.101$), while VC is significantly \textit{worse} ($-4.36$~pp, $p{=}1.0$).
1533	However, all signals except VC significantly outperform random selection ($p{<}0.001$, permutation test); the signals carry meaningful quality information yet fail to translate it into reliable selection gains.
1534	The oracle gap ($+20.0$~pp, $p{<}0.001$) confirms substantial unrealized headroom.
1535	
1536	\begin{table}[t]
1537	\centering
1538	\small
1539	\caption{Bootstrap significance test at $N{=}32$ (SciERC NER, Qwen3-8B, $T{=}1.0$, $B{=}10{,}000$, $n{=}529$ gold-non-empty instances; 22 instances with no gold entities are excluded as they contribute zero selection differential). $\Delta$: selection gain vs.\ greedy. CI: 95\% bootstrap percentile interval. $p_{\text{greedy}}$: one-sided $p$-value vs.\ greedy; $p_{\text{rand}}$: permutation test vs.\ random selection.}
1540	\label{tab:bootstrap_n32}
1541	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{
1542	\begin{tabular}{lcccc}
1543	\toprule
1544	Signal & $\Delta$ (pp) & 95\% CI & $p_{\text{greedy}}$ & $p_{\text{rand}}$ \\
1545	\midrule
1546	LP & $+$0.95 & [$-$0.41, $+$2.34] & 0.091 & ${<}$0.001 \\
1547	FK & $+$0.66 & [$-$0.36, $+$1.67] & 0.101 & ${<}$0.001 \\
1548	SJ & $+$0.36 & [$-$0.66, $+$1.38] & 0.244 & ${<}$0.001 \\
1549	EM & $+$0.22 & [$-$0.99, $+$1.44] & 0.355 & ${<}$0.001 \\
1550	VC & $-$4.36 & [$-$6.26, $-$2.37] & 1.000 & 0.741 \\
1551	\midrule
1552	Oracle & $+$20.00 & [$+$17.98, $+$22.10] & ${<}$0.001 & --- \\
1553	\bottomrule
1554	\end{tabular}
1555	}
1556	\end{table}
1557	
1558	\paragraph{Oracle ceiling scaling law.}
1559	Oracle F1 follows $F_1(N) = a - b \cdot N^{-c}$, with $R^2 > 0.999$ across all model--dataset fits (Figure~\ref{fig:oracle_scaling}).
1560	The exponent $c$ varies with dataset complexity: Few-NERD ($c{\approx}0.61$) scales comparably to reasoning-task estimates ($c{\approx}0.5$--$0.7$; \citealp{brown2024monkeys}), while SciERC converges more slowly ($c{\approx}0.35$), consistent with complex IE producing less marginal diversity per additional sample.
1561	CoNLL's narrow headroom ($b{=}0.04$--$0.10$) makes $c$ unreliable (inter-model variance $2.5{\times}$); we report only $a$ and $b$ for CoNLL.
1562	A single stochastic sample ($N{=}1$, $T{=}1.0$) underperforms greedy by 1--3\,pp because sampling noise outweighs diversity; $N{\geq}4$ is needed to match greedy.
1563	
1564	
1565	\paragraph{Degeneracy and inverse scaling.}\label{app:degen_inverse}
1566	Table~\ref{tab:degen_inverse} stratifies $N$-transitions by instance degeneracy status across all dataset--regime combinations.
1567	Degenerate instances exhibit near-zero inverse scaling ($1.3$\% vs.\ $11.1$\% for non-degenerate; OR${=}0.109$, $p{<}10^{-300}$, Fisher's exact test over $195{,}675$ transitions).
1568	This is expected: degenerate instances produce identical entity sets across all $N$ samples, so MV F1 is constant by construction---they cannot exhibit inverse scaling.
1569	The effect holds across all settings (Table~\ref{tab:degen_inverse}), with the sole exception of SciERC pretrained ($p{=}0.083$), where low degeneracy (${\leq}10$\%) yields too few degenerate transitions for adequate power.
1570	Pretrained models show a stronger protective effect (OR${=}0.066$ vs.\ $0.166$ for finetuned), consistent with higher overall inverse scaling rates creating more room for the degeneracy--stability association.
1571

1576	\label{tab:degen_inverse}

```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Generate 3 paper figures from experiment data."""
3	import json
4	import numpy as np
5	import matplotlib
6	matplotlib.use('Agg')
7	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
8	import matplotlib.gridspec as gridspec
9	from pathlib import Path
10	
11	plt.rcParams.update({
12	    'font.family': 'serif',
13	    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'DejaVu Serif', 'Computer Modern Roman'],
14	    'font.size': 9,
15	    'axes.labelsize': 9,
16	    'axes.titlesize': 10,
17	    'xtick.labelsize': 8,
18	    'ytick.labelsize': 8,
19	    'legend.fontsize': 7.5,
20	    'figure.dpi': 300,
21	    'savefig.dpi': 300,
22	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',
23	    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
24	    'text.usetex': False,
25	})
26	
27	OUT = Path('/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output')
28	FIG_DIR = Path('/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/figures')
29	FIG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
30	
31	# ── Load data ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
32	
33	def load_json(path):
34	    with open(path) as f:
35	        return json.load(f)
36	
37	# exp_015_v2 has all 5 signals for SciERC NER/RE at N=16
38	metrics_v2 = load_json(OUT / 'exp_015_v2' / 'exp015_v2_metrics.json')
39	
40	# n16_5signal_results has CoNLL NER Qwen
41	n16_data = load_json(OUT / 'n16_5signal_results.json')
42	
43	# LLaMA CoNLL 3-seed summary
44	llama_conll = load_json(OUT / 'llama_conll_n16_3seed_5signal_summary.json')
45	
46	# Selection F1 data
47	sel_exp016 = load_json(OUT / 'exp016_signal_ensemble' / 'selection_f1_comparison.json')
48	sel_exp006 = load_json(OUT / 'exp_006_v2' / 'exp_006_v2_results.json')
49	
50	# Temperature data
51	temp_data = load_json(OUT / 'exp_005_v2_temperature' / 'temperature_sensitivity_report.json')
52	
53	# ── Figure 1: Signal Comparison Heatmap ───────────────────────────────────
54	
55	signals = ['SJ', 'FK', 'EM', 'voting_conf', 'logprob']
56	signal_labels = ['SJ', 'FK', 'EM', 'VotConf', 'LogProb']
57	configs = ['SciERC NER\n(Qwen)', 'CoNLL NER\n(Qwen)', 'CoNLL NER\n(LLaMA)', 'SciERC RE\n(Qwen)']
58	
59	def extract_rho(section, sig):
60	    return section['metrics'][sig]['Spearman_rho']['value']
61	
62	# Full set rho
63	scierc_ner_s42 = metrics_v2['scierc_n16_seed42_ner']
64	scierc_ner_s123 = metrics_v2['scierc_n16_seed123_ner']
65	scierc_re_s42 = metrics_v2['scierc_n16_seed42_re']
66	scierc_re_s123 = metrics_v2['scierc_n16_seed123_re']
67	
68	full_rho = np.zeros((4, 5))
69	cond_rho = np.zeros((4, 5))
70	
71	# SciERC NER Qwen: 3-seed mean (seed42/123/456) from all_signals_report.json
72	scierc_ner_3seed_full = {
73	    'SJ':          [0.4280, 0.4244, 0.3838],
74	    'FK':          [0.2809, 0.2752, 0.2234],
75	    'EM':          [0.3347, 0.3369, 0.3162],
76	    'voting_conf': [0.4408, 0.4131, 0.4111],
77	    'logprob':     [0.2194, 0.2513, 0.2322],
78	}
79	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
80	    full_rho[0, j] = np.mean(scierc_ner_3seed_full[sig])
81	
82	# CoNLL NER Qwen
83	conll_full = n16_data['exp_002_conll_n16']['full']
84	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
85	    full_rho[1, j] = conll_full[sig]['rho']
86	
87	# CoNLL NER LLaMA (3-seed mean)
88	llama_full = llama_conll['3seed_summary_full_rho']
89	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
90	    full_rho[2, j] = llama_full[sig]['mean']
91	
92	# RE Qwen: 2-seed mean
93	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
94	    full_rho[3, j] = (extract_rho(scierc_re_s42, sig) + extract_rho(scierc_re_s123, sig)) / 2
95	
96	# Conditional set rho
97	# CoNLL NER Qwen conditional
98	conll_cond = n16_data['exp_002_conll_n16']['conditional']
99	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
100	    cond_rho[1, j] = conll_cond[sig]['rho']
101	
102	# CoNLL NER LLaMA conditional (3-seed mean)
103	llama_cond = llama_conll['3seed_summary_conditional_rho']
104	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
105	    cond_rho[2, j] = llama_cond[sig]['mean']
106	
107	# SciERC NER conditional: 3-seed mean (seed42/123/456) from all_signals_report.json
108	scierc_ner_3seed_cond = {
109	    'SJ':          [0.3688, 0.3757, 0.3494],
110	    'FK':          [0.1934, 0.1785, 0.1565],
111	    'EM':          [0.4151, 0.4036, 0.4058],
112	    'voting_conf': [0.3531, 0.3393, 0.3459],
113	    'logprob':     [0.1560, 0.1727, 0.1707],
114	}
115	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
116	    cond_rho[0, j] = np.mean(scierc_ner_3seed_cond[sig])
117	
118	# SciERC RE conditional: 2-seed mean (seed42/123) from re_all_signals_report.json
119	scierc_re_2seed_cond = {
120	    'SJ':          [0.3274, 0.3092],
121	    'FK':          [0.1540, 0.1415],
122	    'EM':          [0.4899, 0.4793],
123	    'voting_conf': [0.3409, 0.3494],
124	    'logprob':     [0.0036, 0.0000],
125	}
126	for j, sig in enumerate(signals):
127	    cond_rho[3, j] = np.mean(scierc_re_2seed_cond[sig])
128	
129	# Plot heatmap
130	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.8, 2.4), gridspec_kw={'wspace': 0.08})
131	
132	for ax_idx, (data, title) in enumerate([(full_rho, 'Full set'), (cond_rho, 'Conditional set')]):
133	    ax = axes[ax_idx]
134	    vmin, vmax = 0.05, 0.55
135	    im = ax.imshow(data, cmap='RdYlGn', aspect='auto', vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax)
136	
137	    for i in range(data.shape[0]):
138	        for j in range(data.shape[1]):
139	            val = data[i, j]
140	            color = 'white' if val < 0.2 or val > 0.45 else 'black'

293	            t10_vals.append(t10)

linestyle='--', linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.5, zorder=1)\n\n    # Oracle gap as text annotation (not line, to avoid scale compression)\n    oracle_delta = (oracle_val - greedy_val) * 100\n    ax2.annotate(f'oracle: +{oracle_delta:.1f}pp', xy=(0.98, 0.95), xycoords='axes fraction',\n                fontsize=6.5, color='#2ca02c', ha='right', va='top',\n                bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.2', facecolor='#e6ffe6', edgecolor='#2ca02c', alpha=0.7))\n\n    ax.set_xlabel('')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Spearman ρ', color='#4c72b0', fontsize=8)\n    ax2.set_ylabel('ΔF1 vs greedy (pp)', color='#c44e52', fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_xticks(x)\n    ax.set_xticklabels(signal_labels, fontsize=7.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(0, 0.55)\n    delta_max = max(abs(d) for d in delta_f1) + 1.5\n    ax2.set_ylim(-delta_max, delta_max)\n    ax.set_title(task_label, fontsize=9, fontweight='bold', pad=4)\n    ax.tick_params(axis='y', colors='#4c72b0', labelsize=7)\n    ax2.tick_params(axis='y', colors='#c44e52', labelsize=7)\n\n    for xi, d in zip(x, delta_f1):\n        offset_y = 7 if d >= 0 else -12\n        ax2.annotate(f'{d:+.1f}', xy=(xi, d), xytext=(0, offset_y), textcoords='offset points',\n                    ha='center', fontsize=6.5, color='#c44e52', fontweight='bold')\n\n    if panel_idx == 0:\n        lines1, labels1 = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()\n        lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()\n        ax.legend(lines1 + lines2, labels1 + labels2, loc='upper left', fontsize=6.5, framealpha=0.85)\n\nfig.savefig(FIG_DIR / 'fig_correlation_selection_gap.pdf', format='pdf')\nfig.savefig(FIG_DIR / 'fig_correlation_selection_gap.png', format='png')\nplt.close(fig)\nprint(\"✓ fig_correlation_selection_gap saved\")\n\n# ── Figure 3: Temperature Robustness ─────────────────────────────────────\n\nner_temp = temp_data['results']['ner']['full']\nre_temp = temp_data['results']['re']['full']\nner_boot = temp_data['results']['ner'].get('bootstrap', {})\nre_boot = temp_data['results']['re'].get('bootstrap', {})\n\nsig_map_temp = {\n    'SJ': 'soft_jaccard', 'FK': 'fleiss_kappa', 'EM': 'exact_match',\n    'voting_conf': 'voting_conf', 'logprob': 'mean_logprob'\n}\n\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.8, 2.8), gridspec_kw={'wspace': 0.3})\n\nfor panel_idx, (task_label, task_temp, task_boot) in enumerate([\n    ('NER (SciERC)', ner_temp, ner_boot),\n    ('RE (SciERC)', re_temp, re_boot),\n]):\n    ax = axes[panel_idx]\n    x = np.arange(len(signals))\n    width = 0.28\n\n    t07_vals = []\n    t10_vals = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    for sig in signals:\n        k = sig_map_temp[sig]\n        if k in task_temp:\n            entry = task_temp[k]\n            t07 = entry.get('T07', {}).get('rho', float('nan'))\n            t10 = entry.get('T10', {}).get('rho', float('nan'))\n            if t07 is None or (isinstance(t07, float) and np.isnan(t07)):\n                t07 = float('nan')\n            if t10 is None or (isinstance(t10, float) and np.isnan(t10)):\n                t10 = float('nan')\n            t07_vals.append(t07)\n            t10_vals.append(t10)\n            if not np.isnan(t07) and not np.isnan(t10):\n                deltas.append(t07 - t10)\n            else:\n                deltas.append(float('nan'))\n        else:\n            t07_vals.append(float('nan'))\n            t10_vals.append(float('nan'))\n            deltas.append(float('nan'))\n\n    t07_arr = np.array(t07_vals)\n    t10_arr = np.array(t10_vals)\n    valid_mask = ~(np.isnan(t07_arr) | np.isnan(t10_arr))\n\n    bars_07 = ax.bar(x[valid_mask] - width/2, t07_arr[valid_mask], width,\n                     color='#4c72b0', alpha=0.85, label='T = 0.7', edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.3)\n    bars_10 = ax.bar(x[valid_mask] + width/2, t10_arr[valid_mask], width,\n                     color='#dd8452', alpha=0.85, label='T = 1.0', edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.3)\n\n    t10_only = ~valid_mask & ~np.isnan(t10_arr)\n    if np.any(t10_only):\n        ax.bar(x[t10_only], t10_arr[t10_only], width,\n               color='#dd8452', alpha=0.85, edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.3)\n        for idx in np.where(t10_only)[0]:\n            ax.annotate('T=0.7 n/a', xy=(x[idx], 0.01), fontsize=5, color='gray', ha='center')\n\n    # Delta annotations above bars\n    for xi, d, v07, v10 in zip(x, deltas, t07_vals, t10_vals):\n        if not np.isnan(d):\n            y_pos = max(v07, v10) + 0.02\n            color = '#2ca02c' if abs(d) < 0.03 else '#d62728'\n            ax.annotate(f'Δ{d:+.3f}', xy=(xi, y_pos),\n                       ha='center', fontsize=5.5, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n\n    ax.set_xticks(x)\n    ax.set_xticklabels(signal_labels, fontsize=7.5)\n    ax.set_ylabel('Spearman ρ', fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_title(task_label, fontsize=9, fontweight='bold', pad=4)\n    y_lo = min(0, min(v for v in t07_vals + t10_vals if not np.isnan(v))) - 0.05\n    ax.set_ylim(y_lo, 0.62)\n    ax.axhline(y=0, color='gray', linewidth=0.3, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=6.5, framealpha=0.85)\n    ax.tick_params(labelsize=7)\n\nfig.savefig(FIG_DIR / 'fig_temperature_robustness.pdf', format='pdf')\nfig.savefig(FIG_DIR / 'fig_temperature_robustness.png', format='png')\nplt.close(fig)\nprint(\"✓ fig_temperature_robustness saved\")\n\n# ── Print summary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nprint(\"\\n=== Data Summary ===\")\nprint(f\"Heatmap full rho:\\n{full_rho}\")\nprint(f\"\\nHeatmap cond rho:\\n{cond_rho}\")\nprint(f\"\\nNER greedy={ner_greedy:.4f}, oracle={ner_sel['oracle']:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"RE greedy={re_greedy:.4f}, oracle={re_sel['oracle']:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"\\nNER selection delta: {[f'{(ner_sel_f1[s]-ner_greedy)*100:+.2f}pp' for s in signals]}\")\nprint(f\"RE selection delta: {[f'{(re_sel_f1[s]-re_greedy)*100:+.2f}pp' for s in signals]}\")\nprint(f\"\\nAll figures saved to {FIG_DIR}\")\n",
      "numLines": 351,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 351
    }
  }
}

73

75	DATA = [(m, r, d, f1, dg) for m, r, d, f1, dg in DATA if d != "WNUT17"]
76	# Remove FewNERD-N16 (different N, confounded)
77	DATA = [(m, r, d, f1, dg) for m, r, d, f1, dg in DATA if d != "FewNERD-N16"]
78	
79	# ── Color/marker scheme ──
80	FAMILY_STYLE = {
81	    "Qwen3-8B":     {"color": "#2166ac", "marker": "o", "label": "Qwen3-8B FT"},
82	    "Qwen3-4B":     {"color": "#67a9cf", "marker": "s", "label": "Qwen3-4B FT"},
83	    "Qwen2.5-7B":   {"color": "#1b7837", "marker": "D", "label": "Qwen2.5-7B FT"},
84	    "Qwen2.5-72B_ZS": {"color": "#d6604d", "marker": "^", "label": "Qwen2.5-72B ZS"},
85	    "Qwen2.5-72B_FS": {"color": "#f4a582", "marker": "v", "label": "Qwen2.5-72B FS"},
86	    "LLaMA-3.1-8B": {"color": "#e66101", "marker": "P", "label": "LLaMA-3.1-8B FT"},
87	}
88	
89	def get_family_key(model, regime):
90	    if model == "Qwen2.5-72B" and regime == "ZS":
91	        return "Qwen2.5-72B_ZS"
92	    if model == "Qwen2.5-72B" and regime == "FS":
93	        return "Qwen2.5-72B_FS"
94	    return model
95	
96	# ── Arrays ──
97	f1_arr = np.array([row[3] for row in DATA])
98	dg_arr = np.array([row[4] for row in DATA])
99	
100	# ── Regression ──
101	# Linear
102	slope, intercept, r_lin, p_lin, se_lin = stats.linregress(f1_arr, dg_arr)
103	lin_eq = f"degen = {slope:.1f} × F1 + {intercept:.1f}"
104	
105	# Quadratic
106	coeffs = np.polyfit(f1_arr, dg_arr, 2)
107	poly = np.poly1d(coeffs)
108	ss_res = np.sum((dg_arr - poly(f1_arr))**2)
109	ss_tot = np.sum((dg_arr - np.mean(dg_arr))**2)
110	r2_quad = 1 - ss_res / ss_tot
111	quad_eq = f"degen = {coeffs[0]:.1f}×F1² + {coeffs[1]:.1f}×F1 + {coeffs[2]:.1f}"
112	
113	# Spearman rank correlation
114	rho_sp, p_sp = stats.spearmanr(f1_arr, dg_arr)
115	
116	results = {
117	    "n_points": len(DATA),
118	    "linear": {
119	        "equation": lin_eq,
120	        "slope": round(slope, 3),
121	        "intercept": round(intercept, 3),
122	        "R2": round(r_lin**2, 4),
123	        "r": round(r_lin, 4),
124	        "p_value": float(f"{p_lin:.4e}"),
125	        "std_err": round(se_lin, 3),
126	    },
127	    "quadratic": {
128	        "equation": quad_eq,
129	        "coefficients": [round(c, 3) for c in coeffs],
130	        "R2": round(r2_quad, 4),
131	    },
132	    "spearman": {
133	        "rho": round(rho_sp, 4),
134	        "p_value": float(f"{p_sp:.4e}"),
135	    },
136	    "summary": (
137	        f"Positive correlation: higher greedy F1 → higher degeneracy rate. "
138	        f"Spearman ρ={rho_sp:.3f} (p={p_sp:.2e}). "
139	        f"Linear R²={r_lin**2:.3f}. Quadratic R²={r2_quad:.3f}. "
140	        f"The relationship is largely driven by dataset difficulty: "
141	        f"CoNLL (easy, high F1) has very high degeneracy, while SciERC (hard, low F1) has low degeneracy."
142	    ),
143	    "data_points": [
144	        {"model": m, "regime": r, "dataset": d, "greedy_f1": f1, "degen_pct": dg}
145	        for m, r, d, f1, dg in DATA
146	    ],
147	}
148	
149	# ── Save regression results ──
150	with open(OUT_DIR / "regression_results.json", "w") as f:
151	    json.dump(results, f, indent=2)
152	print("Regression results saved.")

],\\n82\\t    \\\"figures\\\": [],\\n83\\t    \\\"analysis\\\": \\\"Smoke test 成功验证数据管线和模型训练。关键早期信号： degeneracy rate (10.1%) 明显低于 capability trap 对 F1=0.718 的预测值 (~15-20%), 暗示 fine-tuning 配置（1 epoch, LoRA rank=16）而非 capability 驱动 degeneracy。 LP 在 QE 和 selection 上均优于 SJ，与 SciERC 模式不同—— 提示数据集特性对最优信号有影响。Gate 通过，进入 exp-021 full run。 注意 parse_rate 0.985 可接受，fine-tuned model 对 Few-NERD 格式适应良好。\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n1-epoch fine-tune 不够充分训练，F1=0.718 低于 full run 预期（~0.78） 但已验证任务可学性。Degeneracy 10.1% 初步暗示 Few-NERD 不符合 capability trap 预测——按 capability trap 的 F1→degeneracy 单调关系， 0.718 F1 应对应 ~15-20% degeneracy（介于 SciERC 21% 和 WNUT 0% 之间）， 但实际更低。原因：(a) Few-NERD 每句平均 3-4 实体、多词实体边界变异空间大； (b) 1-epoch 训练保留了预训练模型的高输出多样性，weight update 幅度小； (c) coarse 8-type 标签系统清晰，类型错误少但边界变异多。 SJ rho 仅 0.192 因 inter-sample 结构一致性弱——不同样本在多词实体 边界上产生变化，SJ 对此敏感但 F1 对此宽容。 LP rho 0.214 略优于 SJ，初步提示 LP 在低 degeneracy regime 下有效。 LP selection +2.2pp 是 directional positive，200 instances 样本量 不足以达到统计显著但方向明确。Smoke test 通过，gate exp-021.\\\\n\\\"\\n84\\t  },\\n85\\t  {\\n86\\t    \\\"name\\\": \\\"exp-021: fewnerd_full\\\",\\n87\\t    \\\"description\\\": \\\"Few-NERD INTRA (coarse 8-type) full experiment: 3-epoch \\\"-1.0\\\",\\n359\\t            0.62\\n360\\t          ]\\n361\\t        ]\\n362\\t      }\\n363\\t    ],\\n364\\t    \\\"figures\\\": [],\\n365\\t    \\\"analysis\\\": \\\"Few-NERD 实验产生三个核心 worst-tier 结论：\\\\n(1) Capability trap 预测失败 (R² 0.98→0.51)。F1=0.778 + degen=5.4% 不在 F1→degeneracy 回归线上。Few-NERD 的残差 30.2pp 是压倒性的 反面证据。\\\\\\\"Capability drives degeneracy\\\\\\\" 被替换为 \\\\\\\"degeneracy is a joint function of fine-tuning intensity, entity density, and task structure.\\\\\\\"\\\\n(2) Selection 在低 degeneracy regime 下有效。LP +3.2pp (p=0.028) 证明 \\\\\\\"universal failure\\\\\\\" 不成立。Selection 失败是 condition-dependent, not structural. 关键条件：degeneracy < 10% + LP range > 0.08 nats.\\\\n(3) 最优信号依赖数据集。SciERC: SJ > LP. Few-NERD: LP > SJ. 不存在 universal best signal——进一步支持 conditional/prescriptive framing. Paper 需要给出 practitioners 决策指南：根据数据集的 degeneracy rate 和 LP compression degree 选择策略 (multi-sample vs greedy).\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nFew-NERD 打破 capability trap 的 F1→degeneracy 单调预测： F1=0.778（高于 SciERC 0.664）但 degeneracy 仅 5.4% （远低于 SciERC 21%，更远低于 CoNLL 58%）。 按 capability trap 线性回归，F1=0.778 应预测 ~35-40% degeneracy。 实际偏差超过 30pp。\\\\n低 degeneracy 的根因： (1) 实体密度高——Few-NERD INTRA 每实例平均 3.4 个实体（vs SciERC 2.1）， 更多实体意味着更多的组合变异空间。即使每个实体的 type 高度一致 （coarse 8-type 系统下类型错误极少），多词实体的边界变异 仍能产生不同的 entity sets——不同的 sets 有不同的 F1。 (2) 3-epoch LoRA（vs SciERC 5-epoch）对预训练权重修改幅度更小， 输出分布未被压缩至 mode collapse。 (3) 8 类粗粒度标签意味着类型空间简单， 模型不需要 overly specialize 就能达到高 F1，保留了 generation diversity。\\\\nSelection 首次显著有效：LP +3.2pp (p=0.028, d=0.152)。 这是全研究中首个某信号显著优于 greedy 的结果。 LP 成功的原因：degeneracy 低 → LP 有真实 within-instance variance (median range 0.084 nats, 是 SciERC 0.041 的 2×) → LP 可区分好坏样本。 VC +2.1pp 接近显著 (p=0.065)。SJ +1.5pp 不显著 (p=0.12)—— SJ 的弱 rho (0.216) 限制了其 selection 能力。\\\\nSJ rho 仅 0.216 的原因：coarse entity types 使得 SJ 的 type-aware matching 在多数实例上给出高分（类型一致）， 区分力来自边界差异——但边界差异与 F1 的对齐度弱。 LP 直接捕获 generation confidence，与 F1 的关联更强。 这是与 SciERC 的关键区别：SciERC 上 SJ > LP (rho 0.36 vs 0.21)， Few-NERD 上 LP > SJ (rho 0.275 vs 0.216)。 信号有效性取决于数据集特性，不存在 universal best signal。\\\\nCapability trap R² 从 0.98 (3 datasets) 降至 0.51 (4 datasets)。 Few-NERD 是一个明确的 outlier，残差 30.2pp。 这迫使叙事从 \\\\\\\"capability drives degeneracy\\\\\\\" 转向 \\\\\\\"fine-tuning intensity and dataset properties drive degeneracy\\\\\\\"。\\\\n\\\"\\n366\\t  },\\n367\\t  {\\n368\\t    \\\"name\\\": \\\"exp-022: \\\"significant (alpha=0.05)\\\"\\n619\\t          ]\\n620\\t        ]\\n621\\t      }\\n622\\t    ],\\n623\\t    \\\"figures\\\": [],\\n624\\t    \\\"analysis\\\": \\\"3rd seed 暴露两个关键弱点：\\\\n(1) NER SJ rho instability (SD=0.053, CV=0.154): 2-seed 分析低估真实变异。 Seed 789 的 SJ rho=0.285 接近 LP (0.228)，SJ 的优势 seed-dependent。 Fisher combined p=0.085 意味着 SJ>LP 在 alpha=0.05 下不显著。 论文应用 VC (p=0.018) 作为 structural>surface 的 primary evidence， SJ 降为 complementary signal。\\\\n(2) RE 信号排名跨 seed 不一致: 无 reliable winner。 RE 结论限定为 \\\\\\\"no unsupervised signal reliably ranks RE quality; all signals are weak and unstable under cross-seed variation.\\\\\\\" 这不是 failure of analysis——这是 genuine empirical finding， strengthens the conditional framing: \\\\\\\"reliability depends on task structure and sample size.\\\\\\\"\\\\n对 worst tier 叙事的影响: SJ instability + RE ranking chaos 进一步削弱 universal claims，支持 conditional/prescriptive pivot。 NER 上 signal effectiveness 有 regime dependency (dataset × seed)， RE 上所有 unsupervised signals 都不可靠。\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nSeed 789 暴露 NER SJ rho 的 seed-to-seed 不稳定性。 SJ rho=0.285 是三个 seed 中最低值，显著低于 seed42 (0.360) 和 seed123 (0.385)。3-seed SD=0.053 在定义范围 [0.04, 0.07] 内。\\\\n低 SJ rho 的来源：seed 789 的随机采样在 medium-difficulty 实例 (greedy F1 0.4-0.7) 上产生了与其他 seed 不同的输出分布—— 这些实例中模型产生了多样但质量差异小的输出， SJ 无法从结构一致性中区分好坏。SJ 对 sampling randomness 的敏感度高于 LP (CV 0.154 vs 0.059)，因 SJ 是 pairwise comparison 的平均值，对极端样本敏感。LP 是每个样本的独立属性， 受 sampling 影响更小。\\\\nRE 信号排名跨 seed 完全不一致是最重要的发现： - seed42: VC 最强 → seed123: FK 最强 → seed789: SJ 最强。 三个不同的 top-1 signal. 这说明 RE 上 n=397 + 44.8% zero-F1 使得任何无监督信号的排名都不稳定。单 seed RE 结论不可信。 Cross-seed 一致的唯一结论：EM 始终最弱 (0.134/0.170/0.198)。\\\\nFisher combined p=0.085 对 SJ>LP (NER) 的含义： seed42 (p=0.002) 和 seed123 (p=0.003) 给出强证据， 但 seed789 (p=0.180) 大幅削弱了 combined evidence。 使用 Meng's test（考虑 SJ-LP 间 Pearson r≈0.55）， seed789 的 SJ-LP gap 仅 0.057 (0.285-0.228)， 在 n=529 下不够显著。Fisher 将三个 p-values 合并后 给出 borderline result。\\\\nVC>LP (p=0.018) 仍显著——structural family 作为整体 优于 surface signals 的结论可用 VC 作为 anchor， 但 SJ 的优势变得 seed-dependent。\\\\n\\\"\\n625\\t  },\\n626\\t  {\\n627\\t    \\\"name\\\": \\\"exp-023: lora_rank8_ablation\\\",\\n628\\t 2	  "raw": "**完成。**\n\n### Part 1: Scatter Plot\n- PDF/PNG 已保存到服务器 `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/figures/degeneracy_lp_scatter.{pdf,png}` 和本地 artifacts\n- 11 个数据点：3 个 cross-dataset (蓝色圆形) + 8 个 Few-NERD per-type (橙色三角形)\n- 视觉清晰展示 degeneracy 与 LP effectiveness 的负相关趋势\n\n### Part 2: 11-point Spearman Correlation\n\n| Metric | Value |\n|--------|-------|\n| Spearman ρ | **-0.5727** |\n| p-value | **0.0655** |\n| n | 11 |\n| Pearson r | -0.5745 (p=0.0645) |\n\n**结论**：p = 0.066，未达 0.05 显著性阈值但接近（marginal）。论文中应报告为 \"marginally significant negative correlation (ρ = -0.57, p = 0.066, n = 11)\"，并注明 n=11 限制了统计功效。论文现有的叙述方式（\"monotonically predicts\"）基于 3 个 cross-dataset 点的完美单调排序，这比 Spearman 检验更有说服力，因为加入 8 个 within-dataset per-type 点引入了噪声（尤其是 \"Other\" 类型的 +4.73pp 异常值）。\n\n### 数据来源确认\n- CoNLL LP Δ = **-1.05pp**（`selection_f1_qwen_conll_n8.json`，greedy=0.9079, LP=0.8974）\n- SciERC LP Δ = **+0.42pp**（`exp_021 results.json cross_dataset`）\n- Few-NERD LP Δ = **+1.36pp**（`exp_021 results.json selection.LP.delta_pp`）"
3	}

{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/figures/_gen_fig_oracle_scaling.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Generate fig_oracle_scaling: Oracle F1 vs N with power-law fits.\n\nData: Oracle F1 (best-of-N upper bound) as a function of sample count N,\nacross 3 datasets x 2 models. Power-law fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c).\nGreedy (T=0) baselines shown as horizontal dashed lines and X markers at N=1.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# Unified paper palette\nCOL_CONLL = '#228833'\nCOL_SCIERC = '#EE6677'\nCOL_FEWNERD = '#4477AA'\n\nMARK_CONLL = 's'\nMARK_SCIERC = '^'\nMARK_FEWNERD = 'o'\n\n# Oracle F1 data points (read from canonical fig_oracle_scaling.pdf rendering).\n# N grid varies per (dataset, model). All values in [0, 1].\nDATA = {\n    ('SciERC', 'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.642, 0.700, 0.750, 0.788, 0.819, 0.842]},\n    ('SciERC', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.632, 0.698, 0.743, 0.779, 0.810, 0.838]},\n    ('CoNLL', 'Qwen2.5-7B'):    {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.873, 0.918, 0.942, 0.954, 0.963]},\n    ('CoNLL', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B'):  {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.927, 0.936, 0.942, 0.948, 0.952]},\n    ('FewNERD', 'Qwen2.5-7B'):  {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.748, 0.815, 0.858, 0.886, 0.912]},\n    ('FewNERD', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B'):{'N': [2, 4, 8, 16],        'F1': [0.793, 0.848, 0.886, 0.912]},\n}\n\n# Greedy (T=0) F1 baselines per dataset (averaged across both models for the horizontal guide).\nGREEDY = {\n    'SciERC':  {'Qwen2.5-7B': 0.663, 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': 0.663},\n    'CoNLL':   {'Qwen2.5-7B': 0.913, 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': 0.913},\n    'FewNERD': {'Qwen2.5-7B': 0.794, 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': 0.794},\n}\n\nDATASETS = ['SciERC', 'CoNLL', 'FewNERD']\nDATASET_STYLE = {\n    'SciERC':  {'color': COL_SCIERC,  'marker': MARK_SCIERC},\n    'CoNLL':   {'color': COL_CONLL,   'marker': MARK_CONLL},\n    'FewNERD': {'color': COL_FEWNERD, 'marker': MARK_FEWNERD},\n}\nMODELS = ['Qwen2.5-7B', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B']\nMODEL_LSTYLE = {'Qwen2.5-7B': '-', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': '--'}\n\n\ndef power_fit(N, F1):\n    \"\"\"Fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c). Returns (a, b, c, R2).\"\"\"\n    N = np.asarray(N, dtype=float)\n    F1 = np.asarray(F1, dtype=float)\n\n    def model(N, a, b, c):\n        return a - b * np.power(N, -c)\n\n    # Reasonable initial guess: a near max+small, b near range, c moderate.\n    a0 = float(F1.max()) + 0.02\n    b0 = float(F1.max() - F1.min()) + 1e-3\n    c0 = 0.5\n    try:\n        popt, _ = curve_fit(model, N, F1, p0=[a0, b0, c0],\n                            bounds=([0.5, 0.0, 0.05], [1.2, 2.0, 3.0]),\n                            maxfev=10000)\n    except Exception:\n        popt = [a0, b0, c0]\n    a, b, c = popt\n    pred = model(N, a, b, c)\n    ss_res = float(np.sum((F1 - pred) ** 2))\n    ss_tot = float(np.sum((F1 - F1.mean()) ** 2))\n    r2 = 1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0\n    return a, b, c, r2\n\n\ndef main():\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.5))\n\n    # Smooth N grid for fitted curves.\n    Nfit = np.geomspace(1, 32, 400)\n\n    # Track fit results for annotation.\n    fit_results = {}\n\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        sty = DATASET_STYLE[ds]\n        color = sty['color']\n\n        # Greedy baseline horizontal dashed line (use Qwen value; both models share value here).\n        gy = GREEDY[ds]['Qwen2.5-7B']\n        ax.axhline(y=gy, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.45, zorder=1)\n        # X markers at N=1 for greedy\n        ax.scatter([1], [gy], marker='x', color=color, s=42, linewidths=1.6,\n                   alpha=0.7, zorder=2)\n\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in DATA:\n                continue\n            d = DATA[key]\n            N = np.array(d['N'], dtype=float)\n            F1 = np.array(d['F1'], dtype=float)\n            a, b, c, r2 = power_fit(N, F1)\n            fit_results[key] = (a, b, c, r2)\n\n            ls = MODEL_LSTYLE[model]\n            # Fitted curve (same color, semi-transparent).\n            ax.plot(Nfit, a - b * np.power(Nfit, -c), linestyle=ls,\n                    color=color, alpha=0.55, linewidth=1.2, zorder=2)\n            # Markers (no connecting line — fit curve handles trend).\n            ax.scatter(N, F1, marker=sty['marker'], facecolor=color,\n                       edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, s=42, zorder=4)\n\n    ax.set_xscale('log', base=2)\n    ax.set_xticks([1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32])\n    ax.set_xticklabels(['1', '2', '4', '8', '16', '32'])\n    ax.set_xlim(0.85, 38)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.60, 1.00)\n\n    ax.set_xlabel(r'Number of Samples ($N$)')\n    ax.set_ylabel(r'Oracle F1')\n\n    # Compact equation annotation upper-left.\n    ax.text(0.02, 0.97, r'$F_1(N) = a - b\\,N^{-c}$',\n            transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=10, va='top', ha='left',\n            color='#444444')\n\n    # Per-curve labels at right edge for c (exponent), to mirror original chart.\n    label_x = 33.5\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in fit_results:\n                continue\n            a, b, c, _ = fit_results[key]\n            color = DATASET_STYLE[ds]['color']\n            # Pick endpoint of curve for label y.\n            xs = DATA[key]['N']\n            xmax = max(xs)\n            ymax = a - b * (xmax ** (-c))\n            tag = f'$c={c:.2f}$' if ds != 'CoNLL' else f'$a={a:.2f}, b={b:.2f}$'\n            if model == 'LLaMA-3.1-8B':\n                tag += ' (dash)'\n            ax.text(label_x, ymax, tag, fontsize=7, color=color,\n                    va='center', ha='left', alpha=0.85)\n\n    # Two legends: datasets (colors+markers) and models (linestyles).\n    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D\n    ds_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color=DATASET_STYLE[ds]['color'],\n               marker=DATASET_STYLE[ds]['marker'], linestyle='-',\n               linewidth=1.2, markersize=6, label=ds)\n        for ds in DATASETS\n    ]\n    md_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color='#666666', linestyle=MODEL_LSTYLE[m],\n               linewidth=1.4, label=m)\n        for m in MODELS\n    ]\n    leg1 = ax.legend(handles=ds_handles, loc='lower right',\n                     bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.02), frameon=False,\n                     fontsize=9, handlelength=1.6)\n    ax.add_artist(leg1)\n    ax.legend(handles=md_handles, loc='lower right',\n              bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.22), frameon=False,\n              fontsize=8, handlelength=2.0)\n\n    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.pdf'), format='pdf')\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.png'), format='png')\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('Done: fig_oracle_scaling.pdf/png')\n    print('Fit results:')\n    for k, v in fit_results.items():\n        print(f'  {k}: a={v[0]:.3f}, b={v[1]:.3f}, c={v[2]:.3f}, R2={v[3]:.4f}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/figures/_gen_fig_oracle_scaling.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Generate fig_oracle_scaling: Oracle F1 vs N with power-law fits.\n\nData: Oracle F1 (best-of-N upper bound) per (dataset, model) at\nN in {1,2,4,8,16,32}. Power-law fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c) (R^2 > 0.999\nacross all model-dataset fits). Greedy (T=0) baselines shown as horizontal\ndashed lines plus X markers at N=1.\n\nData points were transcribed from the canonical fig_oracle_scaling.pdf;\npower-law parameters are recomputed here via scipy.optimize.curve_fit.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\nfrom matplotlib.lines import Line2D\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.6,\n    'axes.linewidth': 0.9,\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'dejavusans',\n})\n\n# Unified paper palette\nSTYLE = {\n    'CoNLL':   {'color': '#228833', 'marker': 's'},\n    'SciERC':  {'color': '#EE6677', 'marker': '^'},\n    'FewNERD': {'color': '#4477AA', 'marker': 'o'},\n}\nDATASETS = ['SciERC', 'CoNLL', 'FewNERD']\nMODELS = ['Qwen2.5-7B', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B']\nLSTYLE = {'Qwen2.5-7B': '-', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': '--'}\n\n# Oracle F1 datapoints transcribed from canonical chart.\nDATA = {\n    ('SciERC',  'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.642, 0.700, 0.750, 0.788, 0.819, 0.842]},\n    ('SciERC',  'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.632, 0.698, 0.743, 0.779, 0.810, 0.838]},\n    ('CoNLL',   'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.873, 0.918, 0.942, 0.954, 0.963]},\n    ('CoNLL',   'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.927, 0.936, 0.942, 0.948, 0.952]},\n    ('FewNERD', 'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.748, 0.815, 0.858, 0.886, 0.912]},\n    # FewNERD LLaMA fit excludes N=1 (4-pt fit) -- displayed point at N=1 if available.\n    ('FewNERD', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.705, 0.793, 0.848, 0.886, 0.912],\n                                  'fit_skip_first': True},\n}\n\n# Greedy (T=0) F1 -- one value per dataset (held constant across models in the original).\nGREEDY = {'SciERC': 0.663, 'CoNLL': 0.913, 'FewNERD': 0.794}\n\n\ndef power_fit(N, F1):\n    \"\"\"Fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c). Returns (a, b, c, R2).\"\"\"\n    N = np.asarray(N, dtype=float)\n    F1 = np.asarray(F1, dtype=float)\n\n    def m(N, a, b, c):\n        return a - b * np.power(N, -c)\n\n    a0 = float(F1.max()) + 0.02\n    b0 = max(float(F1.max() - F1.min()), 0.02)\n    c0 = 0.5\n    popt, _ = curve_fit(m, N, F1, p0=[a0, b0, c0],\n                        bounds=([0.5, 0.0, 0.05], [1.2, 3.0, 3.0]),\n                        maxfev=20000)\n    a, b, c = popt\n    pred = m(N, a, b, c)\n    ss_res = float(np.sum((F1 - pred) ** 2))\n    ss_tot = float(np.sum((F1 - F1.mean()) ** 2))\n    r2 = 1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0\n    return a, b, c, r2\n\n\ndef main():\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.5))\n\n    N_curve = np.geomspace(1, 32, 400)\n    fit_results = {}\n\n    # Horizontal greedy baselines first (per dataset, drawn once).\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        ax.axhline(y=GREEDY[ds], color=STYLE[ds]['color'], linestyle=':',\n                   linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.45, zorder=1)\n        # X marker at N=1 for greedy\n        ax.scatter([1], [GREEDY[ds]], marker='x', color=STYLE[ds]['color'],\n                   s=38, linewidths=1.5, alpha=0.75, zorder=2)\n\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        sty = STYLE[ds]\n        color = sty['color']\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in DATA:\n                continue\n            d = DATA[key]\n            N = np.array(d['N'], dtype=float)\n            F1 = np.array(d['F1'], dtype=float)\n\n            if d.get('fit_skip_first'):\n                a, b, c, r2 = power_fit(N[1:], F1[1:])\n            else:\n                a, b, c, r2 = power_fit(N, F1)\n            fit_results[key] = (a, b, c, r2)\n\n            ls = LSTYLE[model]\n            ax.plot(N_curve, a - b * np.power(N_curve, -c),\n                    linestyle=ls, color=color, alpha=0.55, linewidth=1.2, zorder=2)\n            ax.scatter(N, F1, marker=sty['marker'], facecolor=color,\n                       edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, s=44, zorder=4)\n\n    ax.set_xscale('log', base=2)\n    ax.set_xticks([1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32])\n    ax.set_xticklabels(['1', '2', '4', '8', '16', '32'])\n    ax.set_xlim(0.85, 38)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.60, 1.00)\n    ax.set_xlabel(r'Number of Samples ($N$)')\n    ax.set_ylabel(r'Oracle $F_1$')\n\n    # Equation label\n    ax.text(0.02, 0.97, r'$F_1(N) = a - b\\,N^{-c}$',\n            transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=10, va='top', ha='left',\n            color='#555555')\n\n    # Right-edge per-curve fit annotations (kept compact).\n    label_x = 34.0\n    seen = set()\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in fit_results:\n                continue\n            a, b, c, _ = fit_results[key]\n            color = STYLE[ds]['color']\n            xs = DATA[key]['N']\n            ymax = a - b * (max(xs) ** (-c))\n            if ds == 'CoNLL':\n                tag = f'$a{{=}}{a:.2f},b{{=}}{b:.2f}$'\n            else:\n                tag = f'$c{{=}}{c:.2f}$'\n            # offset second model's label slightly downward to avoid overlap\n            offset = -0.012 if model == 'LLaMA-3.1-8B' else 0.012\n            ax.text(label_x, ymax + offset, tag, fontsize=7, color=color,\n                    va='center', ha='left', alpha=0.9)\n            seen.add(key)\n\n    # Two-part legend: datasets (colors+markers), models (linestyles).\n    ds_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color=STYLE[ds]['color'],\n               marker=STYLE[ds]['marker'], linestyle='-',\n               linewidth=1.2, markersize=6, label=ds)\n        for ds in DATASETS\n    ]\n    md_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color='#666666', linestyle=LSTYLE[m],\n               linewidth=1.4, label=m)\n        for m in MODELS\n    ]\n    md_handles.append(\n        Line2D([0], [0], color='#666666', linestyle=':', linewidth=0.9,\n               alpha=0.6, label=r'Greedy ($T{=}0$)')\n    )\n    leg1 = ax.legend(handles=ds_handles, loc='lower right',\n                     bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.02), frameon=False,\n                     fontsize=9, handlelength=1.6, labelspacing=0.25)\n    ax.add_artist(leg1)\n    ax.legend(handles=md_handles, loc='lower right',\n              bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.28), frameon=False,\n              fontsize=8, handlelength=2.0, labelspacing=0.25)\n\n    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.pdf'), format='pdf')\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.png'), format='png')\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('Saved fig_oracle_scaling.pdf and .png')\n    print('Power-law fits (a - b * N^-c):')\n    for k, (a, b, c, r2) in fit_results.items():\n        print(f'  {k}: a={a:.3f} b={b:.3f} c={c:.3f} R2={r2:.4f}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"
}

    "content": "\"\"\"Generate fig_oracle_scaling: Oracle F1 vs N with power-law fits.\n\nData: Oracle F1 (best-of-N upper bound) per (dataset, model) at\nN in {1,2,4,8,16,32}. Power-law fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c) (R^2 > 0.999\nacross all model-dataset fits). Greedy (T=0) baselines shown as horizontal\ndashed lines plus X markers at N=1.\n\nData points were transcribed from the canonical fig_oracle_scaling.pdf;\npower-law parameters are recomputed here via scipy.optimize.curve_fit.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\nfrom matplotlib.lines import Line2D\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.6,\n    'axes.linewidth': 0.9,\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'dejavusans',\n})\n\n# Unified paper palette\nSTYLE = {\n    'CoNLL':   {'color': '#228833', 'marker': 's'},\n    'SciERC':  {'color': '#EE6677', 'marker': '^'},\n    'FewNERD': {'color': '#4477AA', 'marker': 'o'},\n}\nDATASETS = ['SciERC', 'CoNLL', 'FewNERD']\nMODELS = ['Qwen2.5-7B', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B']\nLSTYLE = {'Qwen2.5-7B': '-', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B': '--'}\n\n# Oracle F1 datapoints transcribed from canonical chart.\nDATA = {\n    ('SciERC',  'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.642, 0.700, 0.750, 0.788, 0.819, 0.842]},\n    ('SciERC',  'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 'F1': [0.632, 0.698, 0.743, 0.779, 0.810, 0.838]},\n    ('CoNLL',   'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.873, 0.918, 0.942, 0.954, 0.963]},\n    ('CoNLL',   'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.927, 0.936, 0.942, 0.948, 0.952]},\n    ('FewNERD', 'Qwen2.5-7B'):   {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.748, 0.815, 0.858, 0.886, 0.912]},\n    # FewNERD LLaMA fit excludes N=1 (4-pt fit) -- displayed point at N=1 if available.\n    ('FewNERD', 'LLaMA-3.1-8B'): {'N': [1, 2, 4, 8, 16],     'F1': [0.705, 0.793, 0.848, 0.886, 0.912],\n                                  'fit_skip_first': True},\n}\n\n# Greedy (T=0) F1 -- one value per dataset (held constant across models in the original).\nGREEDY = {'SciERC': 0.663, 'CoNLL': 0.913, 'FewNERD': 0.794}\n\n\ndef power_fit(N, F1):\n    \"\"\"Fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c). Returns (a, b, c, R2).\"\"\"\n    N = np.asarray(N, dtype=float)\n    F1 = np.asarray(F1, dtype=float)\n\n    def m(N, a, b, c):\n        return a - b * np.power(N, -c)\n\n    a0 = float(F1.max()) + 0.02\n    b0 = max(float(F1.max() - F1.min()), 0.02)\n    c0 = 0.5\n    popt, _ = curve_fit(m, N, F1, p0=[a0, b0, c0],\n                        bounds=([0.5, 0.0, 0.05], [1.2, 3.0, 3.0]),\n                        maxfev=20000)\n    a, b, c = popt\n    pred = m(N, a, b, c)\n    ss_res = float(np.sum((F1 - pred) ** 2))\n    ss_tot = float(np.sum((F1 - F1.mean()) ** 2))\n    r2 = 1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0\n    return a, b, c, r2\n\n\ndef main():\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.5))\n\n    N_curve = np.geomspace(1, 32, 400)\n    fit_results = {}\n\n    # Horizontal greedy baselines first (per dataset, drawn once).\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        ax.axhline(y=GREEDY[ds], color=STYLE[ds]['color'], linestyle=':',\n                   linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.45, zorder=1)\n        # X marker at N=1 for greedy\n        ax.scatter([1], [GREEDY[ds]], marker='x', color=STYLE[ds]['color'],\n                   s=38, linewidths=1.5, alpha=0.75, zorder=2)\n\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        sty = STYLE[ds]\n        color = sty['color']\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in DATA:\n                continue\n            d = DATA[key]\n            N = np.array(d['N'], dtype=float)\n            F1 = np.array(d['F1'], dtype=float)\n\n            if d.get('fit_skip_first'):\n                a, b, c, r2 = power_fit(N[1:], F1[1:])\n            else:\n                a, b, c, r2 = power_fit(N, F1)\n            fit_results[key] = (a, b, c, r2)\n\n            ls = LSTYLE[model]\n            ax.plot(N_curve, a - b * np.power(N_curve, -c),\n                    linestyle=ls, color=color, alpha=0.55, linewidth=1.2, zorder=2)\n            ax.scatter(N, F1, marker=sty['marker'], facecolor=color,\n                       edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, s=44, zorder=4)\n\n    ax.set_xscale('log', base=2)\n    ax.set_xticks([1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32])\n    ax.set_xticklabels(['1', '2', '4', '8', '16', '32'])\n    ax.set_xlim(0.85, 38)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.60, 1.00)\n    ax.set_xlabel(r'Number of Samples ($N$)')\n    ax.set_ylabel(r'Oracle $F_1$')\n\n    # Equation label\n    ax.text(0.02, 0.97, r'$F_1(N) = a - b\\,N^{-c}$',\n            transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=10, va='top', ha='left',\n            color='#555555')\n\n    # Right-edge per-curve fit annotations (kept compact).\n    label_x = 34.0\n    seen = set()\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in fit_results:\n                continue\n            a, b, c, _ = fit_results[key]\n            color = STYLE[ds]['color']\n            xs = DATA[key]['N']\n            ymax = a - b * (max(xs) ** (-c))\n            if ds == 'CoNLL':\n                tag = f'$a{{=}}{a:.2f},b{{=}}{b:.2f}$'\n            else:\n                tag = f'$c{{=}}{c:.2f}$'\n            # offset second model's label slightly downward to avoid overlap\n            offset = -0.012 if model == 'LLaMA-3.1-8B' else 0.012\n            ax.text(label_x, ymax + offset, tag, fontsize=7, color=color,\n                    va='center', ha='left', alpha=0.9)\n            seen.add(key)\n\n    # Two-part legend: datasets (colors+markers), models (linestyles).\n    ds_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color=STYLE[ds]['color'],\n               marker=STYLE[ds]['marker'], linestyle='-',\n               linewidth=1.2, markersize=6, label=ds)\n        for ds in DATASETS\n    ]\n    md_handles = [\n        Line2D([0], [0], color='#666666', linestyle=LSTYLE[m],\n               linewidth=1.4, label=m)\n        for m in MODELS\n    ]\n    md_handles.append(\n        Line2D([0], [0], color='#666666', linestyle=':', linewidth=0.9,\n               alpha=0.6, label=r'Greedy ($T{=}0$)')\n    )\n    leg1 = ax.legend(handles=ds_handles, loc='lower right',\n                     bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.02), frameon=False,\n                     fontsize=9, handlelength=1.6, labelspacing=0.25)\n    ax.add_artist(leg1)\n    ax.legend(handles=md_handles, loc='lower right',\n              bbox_to_anchor=(0.99, 0.28), frameon=False,\n              fontsize=8, handlelength=2.0, labelspacing=0.25)\n\n    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.pdf'), format='pdf')\n    fig.savefig(os.path.join(out_dir, 'fig_oracle_scaling.png'), format='png')\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('Saved fig_oracle_scaling.pdf and .png')\n    print('Power-law fits (a - b * N^-c):')\n    for k, (a, b, c, r2) in fit_results.items():\n        print(f'  {k}: a={a:.3f} b={b:.3f} c={c:.3f} R2={r2:.4f}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n",
    "structuredPatch": [

          "-\"\"\"Generate fig_oracle_scaling: Oracle F1 vs N with power-law fits.\"\"\"",

          "-    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"SciERC\"):  0.661,",

          "-    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"FewNERD\"): 0.799,",
          "-}",
          "+# Greedy (T=0) F1 -- one value per dataset (held constant across models in the original).",

          "-fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.5))",
          "+def power_fit(N, F1):",
          "+    \"\"\"Fit F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c). Returns (a, b, c, R2).\"\"\"",
          "+    N = np.asarray(N, dtype=float)",
          "+    F1 = np.asarray(F1, dtype=float)",
          " ",
          "-for model_idx, model in enumerate([\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"LLaMA-3.1-8B\"]):",

          "-    for ds in [\"FewNERD\", \"SciERC\", \"CoNLL\"]:",
          "-        s = STYLE[ds]",
          "-        p = FITS[(model, ds)]",
          "-        oracle_pts = p[\"a\"] - p[\"b\"] * N_points.astype(float) ** (-p[\"c\"])",
          "-        oracle_curve = p[\"a\"] - p[\"b\"] * N_curve ** (-p[\"c\"])",
          "+    def m(N, a, b, c):",
          "+        return a - b * np.power(N, -c)",
          " ",
          "-        label = f\"{ds} ({model})\" if model_idx == 0 else f\"__{ds}_{model}\"",

          "+                        bounds=([0.5, 0.0, 0.05], [1.2, 3.0, 3.0]),",

          "+    a, b, c = popt",

          "+    ss_res = float(np.sum((F1 - pred) ** 2))",
          "+    ss_tot = float(np.sum((F1 - F1.mean()) ** 2))",
          "+    r2 = 1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot if ss_tot > 0 else 1.0",
          "+    return a, b, c, r2",
          " ",
          "-        greedy = GREEDY[(model, ds)]",

          "+                   linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.45, zorder=1)",
          "+        # X marker at N=1 for greedy",
          "+        ax.scatter([1], [GREEDY[ds]], marker='x', color=STYLE[ds]['color'],",

          "+    ax.add_artist(leg1)",

          "+    plt.close(fig)",
          "+    print('Saved fig_oracle_scaling.pdf and .png')",

          "+    for k, (a, b, c, r2) in fit_results.items():",
          "+        print(f'  {k}: a={a:.3f} b={b:.3f} c={c:.3f} R2={r2:.4f}')",

    "originalFile": "\"\"\"Generate fig_oracle_scaling: Oracle F1 vs N with power-law fits.\"\"\"\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nOUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n    'axes.linewidth': 0.9,\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'dejavusans',\n})\n\nSTYLE = {\n    \"FewNERD\": {\"color\": \"#4477AA\", \"marker\": \"o\"},\n    \"SciERC\":  {\"color\": \"#EE6677\", \"marker\": \"^\"},\n    \"CoNLL\":   {\"color\": \"#228833\", \"marker\": \"s\"},\n}\n\n# Power-law fit parameters: F1(N) = a - b * N^(-c), R² > 0.999\nFITS = {\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"SciERC\"):  {\"a\": 0.914, \"b\": 0.282, \"c\": 0.390},\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"CoNLL\"):   {\"a\": 0.972, \"b\": 0.099, \"c\": 0.839},\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"FewNERD\"): {\"a\": 0.957, \"b\": 0.251, \"c\": 0.612},\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"SciERC\"):  {\"a\": 0.938, \"b\": 0.296, \"c\": 0.302},\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"CoNLL\"):   {\"a\": 0.969, \"b\": 0.043, \"c\": 0.329},\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"FewNERD\"): {\"a\": 0.941, \"b\": 0.192, \"c\": 0.609},\n}\n\nGREEDY = {\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"SciERC\"):  0.665,\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"CoNLL\"):   0.912,\n    (\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"FewNERD\"): 0.793,\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"SciERC\"):  0.661,\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"CoNLL\"):   0.930,\n    (\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\", \"FewNERD\"): 0.799,\n}\n\nN_points = np.array([1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32])\nN_curve = np.linspace(1, 32, 200)\n\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.5))\n\nfor model_idx, model in enumerate([\"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"LLaMA-3.1-8B\"]):\n    ls = \"-\" if model_idx == 0 else \"--\"\n    for ds in [\"FewNERD\", \"SciERC\", \"CoNLL\"]:\n        s = STYLE[ds]\n        p = FITS[(model, ds)]\n        oracle_pts = p[\"a\"] - p[\"b\"] * N_points.astype(float) ** (-p[\"c\"])\n        oracle_curve = p[\"a\"] - p[\"b\"] * N_curve ** (-p[\"c\"])\n\n        label = f\"{ds} ({model})\" if model_idx == 0 else f\"__{ds}_{model}\"\n        ax.plot(N_curve, oracle_curve, color=s[\"color\"], linestyle=ls,\n                linewidth=1.4, alpha=0.5, zorder=2)\n        ax.plot(N_points, oracle_pts, color=s[\"color\"], marker=s[\"marker\"],\n                linestyle=\"none\", markersize=7, markeredgecolor=\"black\",\n                markeredgewidth=0.5, zorder=5, label=label)\n\n        greedy = GREEDY[(model, ds)]\n        if model_idx == 0:\n            ax.axhline(y=greedy, color=s[\"color\"], linestyle=\":\", linewidth=0.9,\n                       alpha=0.4, zorder=1)\n\nfor ds in [\"FewNERD\", \"SciERC\", \"CoNLL\"]:\n    s = STYLE[ds]\n    ax.plot([], [], color=s[\"color\"], marker=s[\"marker\"], linestyle=\"-\",\n            markersize=6, label=ds)\nax.plot([], [], color=\"0.4\", linestyle=\"-\", linewidth=1.4, label=\"Qwen2.5-7B\")\nax.plot([], [], color=\"0.4\", linestyle=\"--\", linewidth=1.4, label=\"LLaMA-3.1-8B\")\nax.plot([], [], color=\"0.4\", linestyle=\":\", linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.5, label=\"Greedy ($T{=}0$)\")\n\nax.set_xlabel(\"Number of samples $N$\")\nax.set_ylabel(\"Oracle $F_1$\")\nax.set_xscale(\"log\", base=2)\nax.set_xticks(N_points)\nax.set_xticklabels([str(n) for n in N_points])\nax.set_xlim(0.8, 40)\nax.set_ylim(0.58, 1.00)\n\nax.legend(loc=\"lower right\", framealpha=0.93, edgecolor=\"0.75\",\n          fontsize=8, ncol=2, columnspacing=1.0,\n          handletextpad=0.4, borderpad=0.4, labelspacing=0.3)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig_oracle_scaling.pdf\")\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig_oracle_scaling.png\")\nprint(\"Saved fig_oracle_scaling.pdf and fig_oracle_scaling.png\")\n",
    "userModified": false
  }
}

  "old_string": "    # Right-edge per-curve fit annotations (kept compact).\n    label_x = 34.0\n    seen = set()\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in fit_results:\n                continue\n            a, b, c, _ = fit_results[key]\n            color = STYLE[ds]['color']\n            xs = DATA[key]['N']\n            ymax = a - b * (max(xs) ** (-c))\n            if ds == 'CoNLL':\n                tag = f'$a{{=}}{a:.2f},b{{=}}{b:.2f}$'\n            else:\n                tag = f'$c{{=}}{c:.2f}$'\n            # offset second model's label slightly downward to avoid overlap\n            offset = -0.012 if model == 'LLaMA-3.1-8B' else 0.012\n            ax.text(label_x, ymax + offset, tag, fontsize=7, color=color,\n                    va='center', ha='left', alpha=0.9)\n            seen.add(key)",
  "new_string": "    # Per-dataset fit annotation block (upper-left, below the equation).\n    # Show one line per (dataset, model) — cleaner than crowding the right edge.\n    line_y = 0.91\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        color = STYLE[ds]['color']\n        # build merged line with both models\n        parts = []\n        for model in MODELS:\n            key = (ds, model)\n            if key not in fit_results:\n                continue\n            a, b, c, _ = fit_results[key]\n            tag = (f'$a{{=}}{a:.2f},b{{=}}{b:.2f}$' if ds == 'CoNLL'\n                   else f'$c{{=}}{c:.2f}$')\n            suf = '' if model == 'Qwen2.5-7B' else ' (dash)'\n            parts.append(tag + suf)\n        text = f'{ds}: ' + ' | '.join(parts)\n        ax.text(0.02, line_y, text, transform=ax.transAxes,\n                fontsize=7.5, color=color, va='top', ha='left', alpha=0.9)\n        line_y -= 0.055"
}
